Failed checks · key content removed (5) · information omitted (4). One rejected candidate can fail more than one check.

Acceptance requires yes on all 5 checks — information omitted, fluent, deletion only, valid in context, key content removed. A no causes rejection; unresolved required-check uncertainty is held for review. Fluency requires natural, grammatical Chinese.

The omission treatment removes source information through deletion. Fluent omissions can be accepted with additional semantic distortion flags. These flags record secondary mistranslation effects without causing rejection. Possible other meaning changed records uncertainty about the secondary effect.

Flags on accepted omissions · recoverable from context (55) · unit clause (14) · unit word (12) · ambiguous reference (9) · possible other meaning changed (8) · other meaning changed (4) · distributed alignment (1). A candidate can have several flags.

  • 1 Kings 13:1–3 · 从犹大 — The deletion removes Judah as the man's point of departure. The remaining Chinese naturally states his arrival at Bethel without specifying where he came from, and the edit consists only of the specified deletion.

The remaining Chinese naturally retains the return, eating, drinking, and burial consequence, with the subject carried over from 13:21. The supplied edit is exactly the indicated deletion.
  • 1 Kings 13:23–25 · 到老先知所住的城里 — 删除仅去掉“到老先知所住的城里”。“就报告了这件事”衔接自然，保留报告事件，却不再说明这些人前往城里，也不再交代报告地点是老先知居住的城。

  • 1 Kings 13:33–34 · 从地上 — The deletion removes the explicit location or spatial scope of the household's eradication. “使他家被铲除，灭绝” remains grammatical: the household is the understood subject of both coordinated outcomes. Destruction remains stated, but removal from the earth is no longer explicit.

  • 2 Chronicles 26:3–5 · 敬畏上帝 — The exact deletion leaves the grammatical description “教导他的撒迦利亚” but removes what Zechariah taught him. The remaining references to seeking God do not explicitly preserve instruction in fearing God.
  • 2 Chronicles 26:6–8 · 迦特、雅比尼和亚实突的 — The exact deletion leaves natural Chinese but removes the identification of the three cities whose walls Uzziah destroyed. The later mention of Ashdod concerns construction, not the identification of the demolished walls.

  • 2 Chronicles 26:9–10 · 在山地和肥沃的田野 — The edit removes the locations of the farmers and vineyard workers while retaining his love of cultivation and his employment of them. 因此雇有农夫和葡萄园工人 is natural with its subject supplied by 他 in the preceding clause. The supplied result uses deletion only.

  • Judges 18:5–6 · 平平安安地 — 删除后只说“你们去吧”，不再明确表达平安出行的祝愿或安慰。“耶和华看顾着你们的行程”仍保留，但并不等于明确说出“平平安安地去”。编辑后的中文自然完整，且仅删除了指定文字。
  • Judges 18:7–8 · 像西顿人一样 — The edit deletes the explicit comparison between the inhabitants’ secure way of life and that of the Sidonians. The remaining Chinese is grammatical, and the later reference to distance from the Sidonians does not preserve this comparison.
  • Judges 18:9–10 · 攻打他们 — 删除后“我们上去！”自然完整，但不再明确提出攻打当地人的行动。后文“去占领那地吧”保留了占领的指令，并不等于明确保留攻打他们这一行动。编辑结果仅删除指定文字，符合原文支持的信息损失。

  • Judges 18:29–31 · 摩西的孙子、革舜的儿子 — Jonathan and his descendants remain explicitly identified as priests, but Jonathan's genealogy is removed. The remaining coordinated subject and predicate are grammatical, and the edit only deletes the supplied span.
  • Judges 9:1–2 · 耶路巴力的儿子 — 删除后句子自然完整，但不再明确说明亚比米勒是耶路巴力的儿子。9:2提到耶路巴力的七十个儿子，并未明确补回亚比米勒与他的父子关系。

  • Judges 9:15–17 · 脱离米甸人的手 — The edit retains the father's fighting and life-risking rescue but removes the identification of Midian as the power from which the audience was delivered. “冒着生命危险救你们” is grammatical and complete. The supplied edit deletes exactly the specified span.
  • Judges 9:21–25 · 在山顶 — The exact deletion preserves a grammatical account of setting an ambush against Abimelech, while removing its hilltop location.
  • Judges 9:21–25 · 从那条路 — The exact deletion leaves the natural construction “他们抢劫所有经过的人”. It preserves the robbery of passersby but no longer explicitly identifies their passage as being along the road.
  • Judges 9:30–33 · 在田间 — 删除准确。“你和跟随你的人要在今晚前来，埋伏”可以自然表达两个相连的行动要求，埋伏不必带地点补语；但具体埋伏地点已被删除。

  • Judges 9:36–37 · 从那地的中央 — The exact deletion leaves the natural statement “有人下来”, while removing Gaal's specification that these people are descending from the center of the land. The route of the other company remains intact.
  • Judges 9:36–37 · 从占卜者橡树那条路 — “还有一队过来” is natural Chinese and still reports another company's approach, but the exact deletion removes its route by the Diviners' Oak.
  • Judges 9:46–49 · 伊勒比利土庙的 — The remaining “就进了内室” is natural Chinese but no longer identifies the inner chamber as belonging to the temple of El-berith. This named setting is explicitly supported by the English.
  • Judges 9:50–53 · 男女和 — 删除“男女和”后，“城中所有的首领都逃进那里，把门锁上，登上楼顶”自然完整，但逃入、锁门及登楼的参与者仅明确包括首领，不再包括原文列出的男女。
Pass with flags (89)
  • 1 Kings 13:1–3 · 奉耶和华的话 (recoverable from context) — The edit removes the explicit statement that his act of crying out was performed by the LORD's word. The surviving attribution “耶和华如此说” identifies the message's source, but does not explicitly state that the act of proclamation itself followed divine direction. “他向祭坛呼喊说” remains natural and grammatical.
  • 1 Kings 13:1–3 · 在你上面， (recoverable from context) — The deletion removes the explicit location of Josiah's sacrifice of the priests. The surviving occurrences of “在你上面” locate the priests' incense burning and the burning of human bones, not explicitly the sacrifice. The edited construction “他必将那些在你上面烧香的丘坛祭司献为祭” is grammatical.
  • 1 Kings 13:6–7 · 对神人 (recoverable from context) — The edit removes the explicit identification of the man of God as the recipient of the king’s request. His subsequent intercession makes that identification recoverable, but the edited speech introduction no longer states it. “王就说” is natural Chinese, and the supplied edit deletes only “对神人”.
  • 1 Kings 13:6–7 · 跟我 (possible other meaning changed) — Deleting “跟我” removes the instruction to accompany the king. “请回家，吃喝休息一下” remains grammatical, and the deletion is exact. The edit also permits a changed destination reading: an invitation to accompany the king home can now sound like an instruction to return to the man of God’s own home.
  • 1 Kings 13:8–10 · 跟你 (possible other meaning changed) — 删除准确，且“我也不会去”自然通顺。原文明确拒绝与对方同行，编辑后仅明确拒绝去；同行对象消失，也可能使拒绝的范围扩大。
  • 1 Kings 13:8–10 · 在这地方 (possible other meaning changed) — 删除准确，且“也不会吃饭喝水”语法完整。编辑后不再明确把拒绝饮食限定于这个地方，可能被理解为更广泛的拒绝。
  • 1 Kings 13:8–10 · 沿原路 (other meaning changed · recoverable from context) — 删除准确，“也不可返回”自然通顺。原指令禁止沿来路返回，编辑后变为禁止返回本身，扩大了禁令范围。13:10仍说明原路及改走另一条路，能帮助恢复原有路线限制。
  • 1 Kings 13:8–10 · 沿着来伯特利的原路 (other meaning changed · recoverable from context) — 删除准确，“于是神人走了另一条路，没有返回”语法自然。原文只否定沿来伯特利的原路返回，编辑后否定返回本身，并丢失伯特利这一明确地点。路线限制可部分由13:9和保留的“另一条路”恢复，但伯特利不能。
  • 1 Kings 13:11–14 · 当天在伯特利 (recoverable from context) — The exact deletion removes the explicit time and location of the deeds reported. The remaining Chinese is grammatical. Bethel remains named as the old prophet’s residence, but that does not explicitly locate the man of God’s deeds there.
  • 1 Kings 13:11–14 · 对儿子们 (recoverable from context) — The exact deletion removes the explicit statement that the prophet addressed his sons. His command and their subsequent compliance remain natural in context, and 他们 can refer back to the sons in 13:12. Their role as addressees is inferable but no longer explicitly stated.
  • 1 Kings 13:23–25 · 为他 (recoverable from context) — 删除确实仅去掉“为他”。“老先知就备好驴”自然完整，但不再明说备驴是为了神人；上下文虽可帮助推知受益者，仍不等于明确陈述这一关系。
  • 1 Kings 13:23–25 · 在路上 (recoverable from context) — 删除仅去掉相遇事件中的“在路上”，剩余句子自然。编辑后仍说明他已经上路以及尸体倒在路上，但不再直接将狮子遇见他的地点限定为路上；该地点可以从上下文推知。
  • 1 Kings 13:26–30 · 神人的 (recoverable from context) — The deletion removes the explicit identification of whose body the old prophet lifted. 尸体 remains grammatical and contextually identifiable through the preceding death and discovery; that recoverability does not restore the deleted identification within this statement.
  • 1 Kings 13:26–30 · 为他 (recoverable from context) — 众人哀哭 is natural Chinese. The deletion retains the mourning event and lament but removes its expressly stated target. The burial context and “我的兄弟” make that target recoverable.
  • 2 Chronicles 26:11–15 · 英勇战士的 (recoverable from context) — 编辑保留宗族首领的数量，但不再明确这些首领属于英勇战士这一群体。“这些宗族首领共有二千六百人”语法自然；前后的军队叙述可以帮助理解其军事关联，却没有在本句明确保留该关系。
  • 2 Chronicles 26:16–18 · 带着耶和华的八十位勇敢的祭司， (ambiguous reference) — Accepted: the deletion removes the eighty accompanying priests and their participation in the entry. “祭司亚撒利雅跟在他后面进去” remains grammatical, with “他” referring to Uzziah. The following verse’s “他们” loses its explicit plural antecedent, but an implicitly understood priestly group remains possible; this reference gap does not make the edited sentence ungrammatical.
  • 2 Kings 11:1–3 · 和他的乳母一同 (recoverable from context) — The exact deletion leaves a natural sentence about Joash's six years in hiding. It removes his nurse's participation in that prolonged temple concealment. Her earlier concealment with him remains explicit in 11:2, making continued companionship inferable but not explicitly stated.
  • 2 Kings 11:4–8 · 到了第七年， (unit clause) — The exact deletion removes the seventh-year setting. The remaining verse begins naturally with 耶何耶大 and retains a grammatical sequence of actions, continuing into 11:5.
  • 2 Kings 11:4–8 · 在安息日 (recoverable from context) — The exact deletion leaves the natural construction 你们上值的人，三分之一要守卫王宫. It removes the explicit Sabbath restriction on the incoming duty group. The Sabbath schedule remains inferable from the complementary off-duty group in 11:7.
  • 2 Kings 11:4–8 · 为王 (recoverable from context) — The exact deletion leaves a grammatical instruction to guard the LORD’s house. It removes the explicit relationship between that assignment and service or protection for the king, although the surrounding instructions retain the royal-protection context.
  • 2 Kings 11:4–8 · 手拿兵器， (unit clause) — The exact deletion produces the natural instruction 你们必须围护君王. Surrounding and accompanying the king remain commanded, but holding weapons is no longer explicitly required. The surviving execution instruction does not itself state that requirement.
  • 2 Kings 11:9–11 · 手拿兵器， (unit clause) — Accepted: the edited sentence naturally describes the guards' position and protection of the king but no longer states that they held weapons. The preceding transfer of weapons does not explicitly preserve that action.
  • 2 Kings 11:15–16 · 在耶和华的殿里 (other meaning changed) — “不可杀她”语法自然，且仅删除了指定地点短语。原文明确禁止在耶和华的殿里杀她；编辑删除该地点限制，也把禁令扩大为不限定地点的禁杀。后一变化记录为非阻断性语义变化；原有地点限制及其丢失均有双语文本支持。
  • 2 Kings 11:17–18 · 守卫耶和华的殿 (unit clause) — “耶何耶大祭司还派护卫” is a natural statement that Jehoiada also dispatched guards. The exact deletion removes their specific assignment to guard the LORD's house.
  • 2 Kings 6:24–25 · 过了一些时候， (unit clause) — The deletion removes the explicit passage of some time before Ben-hadad's military action. The remaining sentence is grammatical, and the supplied edit deletes only the specified span.
  • 2 Kings 6:24–25 · 撒马利亚 (recoverable from context · unit word) — The famine's named location is no longer explicitly stated in 6:25, although Samaria remains recoverable from the siege in 6:24. “于是发生了大饥荒” is a natural Chinese event statement without an explicit location.
  • 2 Kings 6:32–33 · 对长老们 (recoverable from context) — The deletion removes the explicit identification of the elders as the recipients of Elisha’s speech. Their presence and the quoted plural address make that relationship inferable, but the edited speech introduction no longer states it. “以利沙就说” is grammatical, and the supplied edit deletes only the specified text.
  • 2 Kings 6:32–33 · 在他后面， (recoverable from context) — The edited question retains the master’s footsteps but no longer locates them behind the messenger. The earlier statement that the messenger was sent ahead supports inference of this ordering. “不是有他主人的脚步声吗？” remains natural, and the edit is deletion-only.
  • 2 Kings 6:32–33 · 与他们 (recoverable from context) — The deletion removes the explicit statement that Elisha was speaking with the previously mentioned people when the messenger arrived. The preceding verse identifies them as the elders, so the audience remains recoverable. “以利沙还在说话的时候” is grammatical, and only the specified phrase is deleted.
  • 2 Kings 6:32–33 · 耶和华 (recoverable from context · unit word) — The edited question retains the king’s challenge to continued waiting but no longer explicitly identifies the LORD as the one awaited. The surviving reference to the LORD identifies the source of the calamity, a different relationship; it makes the waiting target inferable rather than explicitly stated. “我为什么还要等候呢？” is natural Chinese, and the edit deletes only the specified occurrence.
  • 2 Kings 7:3–4 · 亚兰人的 (ambiguous reference) — The exact deletion leaves a grammatical proposal to go to a camp but removes whose camp it is. The following “他们” can naturally refer to the camp's occupants, although their identity is no longer specified.
  • 2 Kings 7:8–9 · 从那里 (recoverable from context) — The edited sequence is grammatical. It retains entry into another tent and the taking of items, but no longer explicitly identifies that tent as the source of those items. That source remains readily inferable from the preceding clause.
  • 2 Kings 7:8–9 · 彼此 (unit word) — “他们说” is natural Chinese and retains the speakers and their words. Removing “彼此” nevertheless removes the explicit statement that they addressed one another.
  • 2 Kings 7:10–11 · 向城门的守卫 (recoverable from context) — 删除后不再明说他们向城门守卫呼喊，只说他们去呼喊并报告营地的情况。“他们去呼喊，说……”自然、合乎语法，且所给结果仅删除了指定文字。下一节守门人传报消息，使呼喊对象可以从上下文推知，但不替代本句原有的明确说明。
  • 2 Kings 7:12–13 · 对臣仆们 (recoverable from context) — The exact deletion leaves natural Chinese, “王夜里起来，说……”. The king and his speech remain explicit, but the identification of his audience as his servants disappears. A servant’s reply in 7:13 helps recover that audience.
  • 2 Kings 7:12–13 · 城里 (recoverable from context · unit word) — Deleting “城里” produces the grammatical instruction “从剩下的马中带上五匹去吧”. It retains the remaining horses and their number but removes their explicit location within the city. The surrounding discussion makes that location inferable.
  • 2 Kings 7:12–13 · 比留在这里的所有以色列人 (possible other meaning changed · recoverable from context) — The exact result, “他们的处境不会更糟”, is natural Chinese with an implicit comparison standard. It no longer explicitly compares their plight with that of all Israelites remaining here. The following sentence helps recover that comparison, but the edited wording can also mean that their own situation will not worsen.
  • 2 Kings 7:14–16 · ，正如耶和华所说的 (unit clause) — The edit retains the market prices but removes their explicit correspondence to the LORD's word. Both the information loss and the retained statement are supported by the bilingual window. The exact deletion leaves a grammatical Chinese sentence.
  • 2 Kings 7:17–18 · 在城门口 (recoverable from context) — The deletion removes the explicit location of the fatal trampling. The officer's gate assignment makes that location inferable, but does not explicitly locate his death there. The remaining Chinese is grammatical and otherwise preserves the statement.
  • 2 Samuel 21:15–17 · 与以色列 (recoverable from context) — The edit removes the explicit identification of Israel as the Philistines’ opponent. “非利士人再次交战” is grammatical without an expressed opponent; David’s ensuing engagement makes the opposing side recoverable from context.
  • 2 Samuel 21:15–17 · 和我们一同 (other meaning changed) — “你绝不可再出战” is natural Chinese and results solely from the specified deletion. It removes the explicit accompaniment relationship with the speakers. It also broadens the literal prohibition from going into battle with them to going into battle at all.
  • 2 Samuel 21:20–22 · 在迦特 (recoverable from context) — The edit removes the explicit identification of Gath as the battle's location. The remaining existential statement is grammatical; “那里” can refer to the battle's setting without naming it. Verse 21:22 supplies contextual support for recovering Gath.
  • Acts 19:11–12 · 借着保罗的手 (ambiguous reference) — 删除后仍自然地陈述神施行神迹，但不再说明神迹是借着保罗的手施行的。下一节的“他”在所给窗口中失去了明确的保罗这一先行词，造成指代歧义，但不使本句不合语法。
  • Acts 19:13–16 · 向那些被邪灵附身的人 (recoverable from context) — The deletion removes the explicit identification of the people over whom the exorcists invoked Jesus' name. The remaining Chinese is grammatical, although the recipients of the invocation and the quoted command must now be inferred from context.
  • Acts 19:17–20 · 当众 (unit word) — 删除“当众”后，带来书并焚烧的行动仍在，但焚烧发生在众人面前这一事实不再表达。“把自己的书带来，焚烧”可自然理解为共用宾语的连续动作，语法成立。
  • Acts 20:7–9 · 从三楼 (recoverable from context) — Accepted. “便掉了下去” is natural Chinese and retains the fall, but no longer specifies that it was from the third story. The window seat and upstairs room allow a general elevated origin to be inferred, not the exact floor.
  • Acts 23:19–22 · 向我 (recoverable from context) — Deleting “向我” removes the explicit identification of the commander as the report's recipient. “你报告了这件事” is grammatical; the recipient remains inferable from the preceding conversation but is not stated in the edited instruction.
  • Acts 23:23–24 · 今晚 (unit word) — Removing 今晚 leaves a natural time expression, 第三个时辰出发, but no longer explicitly specifies departure at night. This is substantive scheduling information, not merely emphasis.
  • Acts 23:23–24 · 为保罗 (ambiguous reference) — The deletion removes Paul's explicit identification as the person for whom mounts are provided. The remaining Chinese is grammatical, but 他 no longer has an explicit Paul antecedent anywhere in the displayed Chinese window; that referential ambiguity does not make the sentence ungrammatical.
  • Acts 23:31–35 · 连夜 (unit word) — Deleting “连夜” removes the explicitly stated nighttime timing of Paul's transport. The remaining Chinese is natural, and the edit consists solely of the specified deletion.
  • Acts 23:31–35 · 营房 (unit word) — Deleting “营房” removes the stated destination of the infantry's return. “第二天，步兵返回，让骑兵继续护送他。” remains grammatical because “返回” can occur without an explicit destination. No words are added or substituted.
  • Judges 17:1–2 · 对母亲 (recoverable from context) — Accepted. The deletion removes the explicit identification of Micah's mother as his addressee. Her subsequent response makes that identification recoverable, while “他说” remains grammatical and retains Micah as speaker.
  • Judges 17:7–8 · 来自犹大的伯利恒， (recoverable from context · unit clause) — 删除后不再明确说明年轻利未人来自犹大的伯利恒；剩余句子自然完整。17:8仍交代他离开该城，使这项来源信息可从上下文恢复，但不取消本句的信息损失。
  • Judges 17:9–13 · 从犹大的伯利恒来， (unit clause) — The exact deletion removes the man's stated origin in Bethlehem in Judah. The remaining Chinese is grammatical and natural, although his reply no longer directly supplies the location requested by Micah's question.
  • Judges 18:1–2 · 在以色列各支派中 (possible other meaning changed) — Accepted: the exact deletion removes the specified tribal framework of the missing inheritance. The remaining Chinese is grammatical. The edited statement can also sound broader, denying any inheritance rather than specifically an inheritance among Israel’s tribes.
  • Judges 18:1–2 · 都是琐拉和以实陶人， (unit clause) — Accepted: the exact deletion removes the warriors’ named places of origin while leaving a grammatical statement of their selection and mission. Neither town remains elsewhere in the displayed window.
  • Judges 18:11–13 · 基列耶琳以西的 (possible other meaning changed) — The edited topic-comment construction '那个地方，直到今日仍叫玛哈尼但' is natural with a contextually supplied referent. It loses the explicit western location. It may also suggest that Kiriath-jearim itself, rather than a place west of it, bears the name Mahaneh-dan.
  • Judges 18:14–17 · 、一个雕刻的神像和一个铸造的偶像 (recoverable from context) — The exact deletion leaves a grammatical inventory containing the ephod and household gods, but removes two distinct objects from the scouts’ report. Their later appearance in 18:17 does not preserve their explicit inclusion in this statement.
  • Judges 18:14–17 · 也就是米迦的家， (unit clause) — The exact deletion leaves the fluent sequence “来到那年轻利未人的住处，向他问安”. The destination remains the young Levite’s residence, but its identification as Micah’s house disappears.
  • Judges 18:14–17 · 和那六百名带着兵器的人 (recoverable from context) — The exact deletion produces the natural sentence “当时祭司正站在门口”. It removes the explicit statement that the priest stood with the six hundred armed men; it does not assert that he stood alone. Their presence at the entrance is recoverable from 18:16.
  • Judges 18:22–23 · 在后面 (recoverable from context) — The edit retains the calling but removes the explicit statement that the callers were behind the Danites. “他们呼喊，但人就转过身来” is natural Chinese. The pursuit and turning around make the spatial relationship inferable, but do not preserve its explicit expression.
  • Judges 18:22–23 · 对米迦 (ambiguous reference) — The edit removes Micah’s explicit identification as the addressee. “但人就转过身来，说” remains grammatical with the Danites as the subject, but the quoted “你” no longer has an explicitly identified individual referent.
  • Judges 18:27–28 · 到了那平静而毫无戒备的人民那里， (ambiguous reference · unit clause) — The exact deletion removes the explicit encounter with a quiet, unsuspecting population. The remaining Chinese is grammatical: “他们” can be understood as Laish’s inhabitants, although its explicit antecedent is lost and the reference becomes less clear.
  • Judges 18:29–31 · 按着祖先但的名字， (ambiguous reference) — The deletion removes the explicit statement that the city was named after the people's ancestor Dan. The remaining Chinese is grammatical, although the personal referent of the second “但” is less clearly introduced.
  • Judges 18:29–31 · 但支派的 (recoverable from context) — “作祭司” is natural Chinese, but it no longer explicitly identifies the tribe served. The surrounding account of the Danites makes that affiliation inferable without preserving its explicit statement.
  • Judges 18:29–31 · 为自己 (recoverable from context) — The edited relative clause naturally identifies the Danites as those who set up the image, but no longer states that they did so for themselves. That beneficiary relationship is supported explicitly in English and is also recoverable from 18:30.
  • Judges 9:7–8 · 示剑的首领们啊， (ambiguous reference) — Accepted. The deletion removes the audience's explicit identity as the leaders of Shechem. The remaining request and blessing are grammatical, but “你们” no longer has an explicitly identified audience in the displayed window.
  • Judges 9:7–8 · 来统治自己 (distributed alignment · recoverable from context · unit clause) — Accepted. The first statement no longer explicitly specifies that the king is to rule the trees themselves. “要膏立一位王” is grammatical and complete. The relationship remains recoverable from the subsequent request, “请作我们的王,” but that repetition elsewhere does not negate its removal here.
  • Judges 9:7–8 · 我们的 (recoverable from context) — Accepted. “请作王” is a natural request to become king, but it no longer explicitly states whose king the olive tree should become. The earlier purpose clause supplies that relationship contextually; the edited request itself omits it.
  • Judges 9:9–10 · 对无花果树 (ambiguous reference) — 删除后仍是自然的引语句，但不再说明树木向谁发出邀请。前节谈的是橄榄树，不能据此恢复本节新出现的无花果树；读者可能误以为树木仍在对橄榄树说话。
  • Judges 9:9–10 · 我们的 (recoverable from context) — “请来作王”自然且合乎语法，但只明确邀请无花果树作王，不再明说它要作邀请者这些树木的王。该统治关系可以从说话者和上下文推知，但已不在邀请中明确表达。
  • Judges 9:13–14 · 众树 (recoverable from context · unit word) — The edit removes the explicitly identified objects of rule, the trees. ‘去统治吗？’ is grammatical Chinese without an expressed object, but states ruling generally rather than explicitly ruling the trees. The neighboring invitation makes the intended constituency recoverable.
  • Judges 9:15–17 · 你们的 (recoverable from context) — The edit retains the audience's anointing the thornbush as king but no longer explicitly identifies them as the people over whom it would rule. This relationship remains inferable from context. “你们若真心膏立我作王” is grammatical, and the edit deletes only the specified text.
  • Judges 9:15–17 · 为你们 (recoverable from context) — The father still fights and risks his life to rescue the audience, but the fighting is no longer explicitly said to be on their behalf. The rescue makes that connection recoverable without explicitly preserving it. “我父亲曾争战” is natural Chinese, and only the specified span is deleted.
  • Judges 9:21–25 · 因为惧怕他的兄弟亚比米勒， (unit clause) — The exact deletion leaves a natural sequence of fleeing and settling, but removes Jotham’s fear of Abimelech as the reason for living there and the identification of Abimelech as his brother.
  • Judges 9:21–25 · 以色列 (unit word) — The edit exactly removes the name of the realm ruled. “亚比米勒统治三年以后” is natural Chinese and continues into 9:23, but no longer specifies whom or where he ruled.
  • Judges 9:30–33 · 城长 (unit word) — 删除准确，余句自然。西布勒听见迦勒的话而发怒的事件保留，但其城长身份不再明说。
  • Judges 9:30–33 · 以别的儿子 (recoverable from context) — 删除准确，姓名直接充当主语仍然自然。报告中不再明确陈述迦勒是以别的儿子；这一亲属关系虽在9:30保留，仍从本句中消失。
  • Judges 9:30–33 · 明早太阳一出来， (possible other meaning changed · unit clause) — 删除准确。“你就起来攻城”可承接前面的行动指令，语法自然，但不再规定明早日出时攻城。“就”在新语境中也可能被理解为埋伏后随即行动，而原文明确要求等到次日日出。
  • Judges 9:36–37 · 山的 (recoverable from context) — “你把影子看成人了” is grammatical and preserves Zebul's claim of mistaken perception, but no longer identifies the shadows as those of the mountains. The preceding mountain reference makes that association recoverable without explicitly stating it.
  • Judges 9:42–45 · 在田间 (recoverable from context) — The deletion removes the explicitly stated location of the ambush. “分作三队，埋伏” remains grammatical: 埋伏 can stand without a location complement. The surrounding references to the fields make the location inferable but do not explicitly locate the ambush in the edited statement.
  • Judges 9:42–45 · 从城里 (recoverable from context) — The edited wording retains the people's emergence but no longer specifies where they emerge from. “他看见百姓出来” is natural Chinese. The surrounding city-and-fields narrative permits inference of the origin without explicitly retaining it.
  • Judges 9:42–45 · 和跟随他的队伍 (recoverable from context) — The edited sentence explicitly assigns the rush and occupation of the gate only to Abimelech, omitting the accompanying companies as joint participants. It remains grammatical. “另外两队” can refer back to the three companies in 9:43, so it does not create a fluency failure.
  • Judges 9:42–45 · 田间 (possible other meaning changed · recoverable from context · unit word) — The deletion removes the explicit restriction identifying the attacked people as those in the fields. “冲向所有的人，击杀他们” is grammatical. Context can still supply the intended group, but without the restriction, “所有的人” can also suggest a broader set of people.
  • Judges 9:46–49 · 示剑楼里的 (recoverable from context) — The edit removes the explicit identification of the leaders as those in the tower of Shechem. “所有首领听见这事” remains grammatical, and the next verse allows the group’s identity to be recovered.
  • Judges 9:46–49 · 对跟随他的人 (recoverable from context) — The edit retains Abimelech’s speech and instruction but removes the explicit identification of its recipients as his followers. “说” naturally continues the preceding predicates with Abimelech as its subject. The surrounding account makes the recipients inferable.
  • Judges 9:46–49 · 在里面众人的上方 (recoverable from context) — The edited sequence “把树枝堆在内室旁边，放火焚烧” is grammatical. It retains the burning and deaths but removes the fire’s position above the people inside. The people’s presence inside is recoverable from the preceding verses, but the above-them relationship is no longer stated.
  • Judges 9:50–53 · 城里 (recoverable from context) — 删除“城里”后，句子仍自然，但不再明确说明楼位于城内。前后攻城及城民逃入楼中的叙述可以帮助推知这一位置，却没有替代原来的明确定位。
  • Judges 9:50–53 · 城中 (recoverable from context) — 删除“城中”后，“所有的男女和首领”语法自然，其范围可由城市场景推知，但这些逃入者与该城的关系不再明确表述。英文支持原句对参与者范围的限定。
Held for review (0)
    Rejected (5)
    • 2 Kings 7:3–4 · 城里 (recoverable from context · unit word) — In “如果我们说：‘进城去吧’，有饥荒，我们必死在那里”, entering the city supplies the location for the following existential statement, and “在那里” continues that same reference. The result is natural locative ellipsis expressing the same city-famine argument, rather than a distinct omitted circumstance.
    • 2 Samuel 21:20–22 · 身材高大的 — The man's great stature is no longer stated, so this is genuine descriptive information loss rather than emphasis removal. However, the deletion removes only a physical descriptive modifier: the participant, his unusual fingers and toes, his ancestry, and all events remain explicit.
    • Acts 19:17–20 · 这些书的 (recoverable from context) — “他们计算价值”紧接带书、焚书的叙述，仍自然表示计算这些书的价值。删除的是可由当前话题直接补足的定语，没有删除独立事实；五万德拉克马的总价也保留。
    • Acts 23:12–15 · 这场阴谋 (recoverable from context) — “参与的有四十多人” naturally leaves the object of participation implicit: the conspiracy just introduced in 23:12. It still states that more than forty people participated in that conspiracy. This is contextual ellipsis expressing the same statement, rather than loss of a distinct substantive detail.
    • Judges 18:7–8 · 那地 (recoverable from context) — Deleting “那地” leaves a natural implicit-topic continuation: “一无所缺，也没有欺压人的统治者。” The preceding mention of Laish and its inhabitants supplies the setting. The same local conditions remain asserted; only their explicit topic is omitted.
